Заготовка `spec/repo-template/` (repositories 2.2 spec): `just new-repo-draft canvas typescript 3.2 v0.19.12` на клоне `spec` v0.19.12 во временной папке. `.devcontainer/`, `.claude/`, CLAUDE.md, правила RUN.md и раскладка таблиц плана — по ней, где она изменилась, с отличиями, которые держат решения этого журнала.
  4. Коммит перехода говорит, что тег принёс и что взято; записи журнала, которые тег меняет, переписываются на месте (R160 spec); строки таблиц 3 и 4, которые тег закрывает, закрываются.
  5. Переход больше одного ревью — разбить по правилу 11: эта задача — первая часть, остальные — следующими задачами, и назвать их в отчёте.
- **Как проверить:** `just -f spec/justfile docs-check-repo` на новом теге — зелёный; проверки частей, которые задача меняет, — правило 6 этого RUN.md; `/code-review high`, один круг.
- **Мой шаг:** если задача меняет `.devcontainer/` — назвать это в отчёте: после мержа автор пересобирает контейнер, и оркестратор ждёт этого перед следующей задачей.
- **Разделы:** R156 spec, R76 spec; RULES.md spec; repositories 2.2 spec; карточка architecture 3.2 spec.
- **Выяснилось при постановке (2026-11-23):** текст задачи пришёл от оркестратора `spec` (правило 6 COORDINATION.md spec); номер T149 был свободен. Текущего эпика здесь нет — задача вне эпика; ветка — `e22-canvas-2`, первая задача пачки (R159 spec). Тег v0.19.12 несёт одно — строгое правило ссылок (T277, T278 spec): вне плана и архивов — ни номера решения, ни ссылки на раздел или документ, ни номера задачи, только запись `sources` или `measurements` для внешнего факта; по нему переписаны RULES.md spec, CLAUDE.md и RUN.md заготовки, начала `run/` и `docs/` заготовки и комментарии её `.devcontainer/`, а `docs-check` проверяет его по ключу `"citations": true`. Правки CLAUDE.md, правил RUN.md и начал таблиц заготовки — только формулировки ссылок; их берут следующие задачи пачки вместе с остальным текстом. Тексты T139–T148 в `run/TASKS.md` написаны по прежнему R171 spec — «один номер решения, один раз» —, и их правит тот, кто ставит следующие задачи пачки. `.devcontainer/` заготовки отличается от здешнего только комментариями без номеров решений, и по R86 его не трогаем — строка Д16. `.claude/` заготовки — тот же, кроме `deny` R15. `format`, `types`, `contracts`, `legal` и карточка между тегами не менялись; новых строк таблицы 4 `spec` для `canvas` нет; по Д294 spec `LEGAL_FILE` переведён вручную.
- **Сделано (2026-11-23):** ветка e22-canvas-2 — подмодуль, `@altnovatrail/spec` и `@altnovatrail/contracts` на v0.19.12 (`release-verify-npm` принял), `tasks/spec-v0.19.12/legal-3.tar.gz` вместо v0.19.11 (`release-verify` принял, содержимое то же) и `LEGAL_FILE`, тег в CLAUDE.md и RUN.md; `.devcontainer/` не тронут, строк таблиц 3 и 4 тег не закрывает, комментарии `.devcontainer/` — строкой Д16. `docs-check-repo` и `scripts/legal.test.ts` зелёные; остальные проверки и `/code-review high` — у последней задачи пачки (R159 spec).

#### T150. Ссылки по строгому правилу R171 spec: проход по первой части

- **Цель:** то, что первая часть ссылок (по прежнему, узкому тексту R171 spec) уже правила, — комментарии кода, `.proto`, CLAUDE.md, README, `.claude/rules/`, `.devcontainer/` и `docs/README.md`, — без ссылок по строгому правилу R171 spec нового тега; остальное `docs/` — записанные части этого репозитория, которые идут следом.
- **Почему:** слово автора 2026-11-23 после проверки правок; R171 spec переписан строже.
- **Сделать:** по строгому правилу ниже — в этих файлах ни одного номера решения (свои, `spec`, других репозиториев, MathTrail), ни одной ссылки на раздел или документ, кроме одной записи `sources`/`measurements` у внешнего факта, ни одной датированной атрибуции и поправки. Причина — словами, одной фразой, где фраза держалась на ссылке; фраза, которая только отсылала, — убрать. Смысл не менять. План (RUN.md, `run/`) и архивы `docs/plan/` не трогать.
- **Как проверить:** `just -f spec/justfile docs-check-repo` с ключом `"citations": true`, включённым на время прогона, — в этих файлах ошибок нет (ключ включает последняя часть); проверки частей, чей код задача трогает, — зелёные.
- **Разделы:** R171 spec; RULES.md spec.

Слово автора 2026-11-23 после выборочной проверки правок E22 (дословно): «Я категорически не удовлетворён работой. Удаляй отовсюду в коде строчки вида XYZ spec. Чтобы я больше такого не видел. Никаких ссылок на какие-то там решения. Мне всё равно на аргументы, что теряется связь и пр. Истории git достаточно. Далее чтобы я не видел бредовых строчек вида пользователь сказал такого-то числа: (the 2026-11-16 amendment to R24) — нещадно вычищай. Переделывай в каждом репозитории.» Ответы автора на уточнения: убрать всё «… spec» — номера решений и ссылки на разделы и документы `spec`; остаётся одна ссылка на запись `sources`/`measurements` там, где стоит внешний факт (закон, условия провайдера, цена), и в другом репозитории тоже (`sources 2.3 spec`); план — RUN.md, `run/`, COORDINATION.md — и архивы `docs/plan/` не трогать; новый тег, дальше по частям.

**Строгое правило ссылок** (оно заменяет узкое правило R171 spec в его прежнем тексте):
- Ни одного номера решения — ни своего `R25`, ни `R25 spec`, ни `R03 api`, ни MathTrail — в коде и его комментариях, в `.proto` и описаниях схем, в `docs/`, README, CLAUDE.md, RULES.md, `.claude/rules/`, `.devcontainer/`, шаблоне. В журнале решений номер стоит только в заголовке своей записи; запись не ссылается на другие записи.
- Ни одной ссылки на раздел или документ: ни `architecture 3.8 spec`, ни `RULES.md spec, Citations`, ни своих `architecture 2`, `concepts 9`. Исключение одно: утверждение о внешнем мире (закон, условия провайдера, цена, документация чужой программы) ссылается на одну запись — `sources N`/`measurements N`, из другого репозитория `sources N spec`. Открытые вопросы `О-`, `П-`, `Т-` и строки `Д…`/`В…` — ссылка на открытую работу — остаются.
- Ни одной датированной атрибуции и поправки: «the author's decisions of 2026-11-18 in planning T121», «the amendment of 2026-11-16 to R24», «по ответу автора на В60», «решено автором 2026-…» — вычистить; номеров задач вне плана нет. Причина решения — словами; кто и когда решил — история git.
- Комментарий кода — причина словами, без ссылок.
- Вне правила только план — RUN.md, `run/`, COORDINATION.md — и архивы `docs/plan/` (слово автора), и нормативные тексты для авторов, чьи версии в силе не меняются: `legal/` (тексты в силе), проза `format/`, `admission/`, `deployment/`, `consent-age/`, `export/`. Комментарии `.proto` правило касается: правка комментария — не правка контракта.
- Смысл не менять: правка только ссылок и того, что их окружает. Где фраза держалась на ссылке, — сказать причину словами одной фразой или убрать фразу, если она только отсылала.

- **Выяснилось при постановке (2026-11-23):** текст задачи пришёл от оркестратора `spec` (правило 6 COORDINATION.md spec); номер T150 был свободен. Текущего эпика нет — задача вне эпика; ветка — `e22-canvas-2`, задача пачки после T149, не последняя (R159 spec). `.proto` и описаний схем в `canvas` нет. Пробный `docs-check-repo` с ключом назвал в файлах части 149 мест: 88 в CLAUDE.md, README.md, `docs/README.md` и `.claude/rules/`, 61 в комментариях кода, `.devcontainer/` с ним; ещё номера решений и задач стояли в строках кода, которых проверка не читает, — имена тестов, `licenses.json`, `vectors/pages.json`, — их правило тоже касается («в коде»). `.devcontainer/` по R86 брал правку заготовки только больше комментария (строка Д16): решено исполнителем по рекомендации — строка В87.
- **Сделано (2026-11-23):** ветка e22-canvas-2 — в файлах части убраны 88 ссылок в документах и 166 в коде: номера решений и задач в комментариях, именах тестов, `licenses.json` и `vectors/pages.json`, ссылки на разделы `spec` — словами или именем файла (`spec/docs/architecture.md`, глоссарий `spec`); CLAUDE.md о ссылках — по CLAUDE.md заготовки; комментарии `.devcontainer/` — заготовки целиком, R86 берёт комментарий, который нарушает правило ссылок, Д16 закрыта (В87, решено исполнителем по рекомендации); `docs-check-repo` с ключом не называет в файлах части ни одного места, без ключа — зелёный; `just ci-lint` и тесты Node зелёные; `/code-review high` и pull request — в последней задаче пачки.

#### T139. Ссылки по правилу R171 spec: журнал решений, от начала до «Detailing — continued»

- **Цель:** в разделах `##` журнала `docs/decisions.md` от «`spec` tag» до «Detailing — continued» включительно ссылка стоит, только если читатель пойдёт по ней, чтобы действовать (R171 spec), и нет датированных поправок: каждая свёрнута в действующий текст своей записи.
- **Почему:** R171 spec; строка 7.45 COORDINATION.md spec. Часть задачи T138, разбитой по правилу 11 RUN.md и шагу 9 её текста: T138 — план, CLAUDE.md, `.claude/rules/` и комментарии кода; журнал решений — T139–T146 по своим разделам `##`; `docs/architecture.md` — T147; `docs/sources.md`, `docs/measurements.md` и ключ — T148. Объём — около 19 тыс. слов, 44 поправок.
- **Зависит от:** T138.
- **Сделать:**
  1. Каждую датированную поправку раздела — абзац «— *…Amendment <дата>…*» и ссылку вида «the 2026-10-21 amendment to R25 spec» — свернуть в действующий текст записи, на месте: «Почему», отвергнутое и цитаты источников сохранить (R160 spec). Хвост «— *Consequence:*» — список файлов и разделов, которые правит изменение решения, без номеров задач. Ссылку на поправку записи `spec` — заменить ссылкой на запись.
  2. Каждая ссылка раздела — по правилу: внешний факт, который можно оспорить, — одна запись `sources`/`measurements`; решённый выбор, который выглядит изменяемым, — один номер решения, один раз в тексте; строки «Д…» и «В…» — можно; номер задачи — заменить файлом, решением или словами; остальное — убрать; в скобке не больше двух ссылок; цепочку — свести к одному месту, где утверждение решено.
  3. Смысл не менять: правка — только ссылок и того, что их окружает, и свёртка поправок. Запись, которая при свёртке оказалась противоречивой, или ссылка, о которой есть настоящее сомнение, — строка таблицы 3 (`run/QUESTIONS.md`), не правка наугад.
  4. Остальные разделы журнала не трогать: их берут свои части. Ключ `"citations": true` не включать — его включает T148.
  5. В описании PR — таблица «поправка → место в новом тексте», для ревью.
- **Как проверить:** `just -f spec/justfile docs-check-repo` — зелёный; тот же запуск с временно включённым ключом `"citations": true` в `docs-budgets.json` не называет ни одного места в разделах этой части (ключ вернуть); `/code-review high`, один круг.
- **Разделы:** R171 spec, R160 spec; RULES.md spec — The bar и Citations; `spec/repo-template/files/docs/decisions.md`; свой `docs/decisions.md`, разделы этой части.
- **Выяснилось при выполнении (2026-11-23):** тело задачи написано по прежнему, узкому тексту R171 spec; на теге v0.19.12 правило строже, и задача сделана по строгому правилу, как его даёт тело T150 выше, в границах своего тела — записи R01–R11, свёртка поправок и хвост «— *Consequence:*» списком файлов, как сказано. Пробный `docs-check-repo` с ключом назвал в разделах части 761 место: 282 номера задач, 267 номеров решений, 123 ссылки на разделы, 15 на документы, 73 датированные атрибуции и поправки, одну скобку с тремя ссылками; датированных поправок — 44 абзаца (R02 — 1, R07 — 16, R08 — 16, R09 — 6, R11 — 5) и семь ссылок на поправки решений `spec` (R01, R03, R09). Записи правились в шести копиях репозитория по записям и собраны в `docs/decisions.md`; за пределами R01–R11 журнал не изменился. При свёртке нашлись места, устаревшие независимо от ссылок: R09 и R11 — что П-10 и П-12 ещё решат, куда идёт файл детского образца (П-10 в `spec` закрыт), R11 — измерение автора по черновику `draft-0` (его убрала поздняя запись), R08 — ступень `toolchain` вместо `toolchain-core`; смысл задача не меняет, и они — строкой Д17. Вопросов автору нет.
- **Сделано (2026-11-23):** ветка e22-canvas-2 — в R01–R11 журнала нет ни номеров решений, кроме заголовков записей, ни номеров задач, ни ссылок на разделы и документы, ни датированных атрибуций: причина — словами, внешний факт — одной записью `sources`; 44 поправки свёрнуты в действующий текст записей, «Почему», отвергнутое и цитаты сохранены, «Consequence» — списком файлов; таблица «поправка → место» — в описании коммита; устаревшие места — Д17. `docs-check-repo` с ключом не называет в R01–R11 ни одного места (было 761), без ключа — зелёный; `/code-review high` и pull request — в последней задаче пачки.

Задачи eval-набора модуля сборка уже берёт из `modules` — копией вывода `module-kit open` релиза v0.2.7 в `tasks/modules-v0.2.7/`, которую `ci-licenses` сверяет с выводом заново (T101, T112; R71), — а замер и сеанс образца ребёнка остаются на копии `tutor` v0.1.22 формата 0.4, по моему решению (R71) | T31 | Страница собирает замер из временного файла и тогда, когда `modules` уже выпускает содержание, а ссылки на задачи в образцах двух источников могут не совпасть: вид задачи в файле `tutor` временный, пока файл не перейдёт на схему R51 spec (поправка 2026-10-26 к R25 spec; [docs/architecture.md](../architecture.md), раздел 3.5) | Своей задачей перехода замера на `modules` — когда замер попросит задачи или медиа модулей, или к следующему замеру. Сборка тогда берёт открытые виды и медиа из пакетов модулей через CLI `module-kit` и не собирает версию, которую индекс новейшего релиза `modules` отметил снятой (R62 spec, R63 spec; R41, R63); релиз `modules`, бинарник `module-kit` и файл `tutor` CI берёт, только проверив подпись рецептом `release-verify` (R77 spec; R44). Сверено 2026-11-12, T110: у `modules` — v0.2.5, у `module-kit` — v0.3.1, у `tutor` — v0.1.24 (`gh release list`), а задачи модулей замер не просил, и долг остаётся. Сверки 2026-11-01 — 2026-11-12 — в прежней редакции строки: [docs/plan/E13.md](E13.md), раздел «Таблица 4 RUN.md до сжатия 2026-11-12» |

Остаётся временное поле задачи `canvas.tools`, которого нет ни в одной версии формата (поправка 2026-10-26 к R51 spec). Тесты держат копии примеров и векторов формата 0.4 — задачи и паспорта в `task.test.ts` и `manifest.test.ts`, уроков в `example.test.ts`; тег в их комментариях — v0.14.0 (поправка 2026-11-08 к R32; R67) | T42, T44 | Копии в тестах расходятся с форматом молча, если переход их не сверит, а `canvas.tools` знает только `canvas` | `canvas.tools` — с минорной версией формата, у которой будет поле инструментов. Каждый переход сверяет копии — `git -C spec diff <старый> <новый> -- format/` — и типы пакета. | Д10 | Firefox не прокручивает к пункту списка рабочей тетради: `scrollIntoView` на его `<g>` без `transform` не двигает контейнер, а на его `<text>` и `<rect>` — двигает; Chromium и WebKit прокручивают к группе. Группа, поставленная `transform`, прокручивается во всех трёх ([measurements 1.5](../measurements.md)). Тест `workbook.test.ts` в Firefox прокручивает к тексту пункта (T111; R78) | T111, `/code-review high` | Читатель экрана или страница, которые в Firefox просят показать пункт, его не покажут, если идут через сам `<g>`: что делает читатель экрана, не проверено (Д7) | Когда пункт ставится заново или трогают его разметку: поставить группу пункта `transform`, а текст и рамки — от её начала, и вернуть тест к группе во всех трёх движках |

Переход на релиз `modules` через CLI `module-kit` — своей задачей, которой в плане ещё нет | T31 | Страница собирается из временного файла и тогда, когда `modules` уже выпустил содержание, а ссылки на задачи в образцах двух источников могут не совпасть: вид задачи в файле `tutor` временный, пока файл не перейдёт на схему R51 spec (поправка 2026-10-26 к R25 spec; [docs/architecture.md](../architecture.md), раздел 3.5) | Своей задачей перехода — когда сборке понадобятся медиа или eval-набор модуля, или к следующему замеру: `modules` с первым содержанием и `module-kit` с CLI открытых видов уже вышли (раздел 3.5). Сборка тогда берёт открытые виды и медиа из пакетов модулей и не собирает версию, которую индекс новейшего релиза `modules` отметил снятой (R62 spec, R63 spec; R41). ## Начало RUN.md до сжатия 2026-11-12

Список «Сделанные эпики» и начала таблиц 2 и 3 RUN.md — дословно, как они стояли до сжатия 2026-11-12, которое автор попросил вне задачи, чтобы сессии тратили меньше токенов на чтение RUN.md. ## Раздел 2 architecture до T129

Раздел 2 [architecture.md](../architecture.md) — дословно, как он стоял до T129, где его подразделы сжались до решённого и того, что ещё открыто (R160 spec). Здесь — что было известно и из чего выбирали, когда решали каждый пункт; решения, их почему и отвергнутое — в журнале. Поправлены только относительные ссылки и заголовки: подраздел назван «Подраздел 2.N», чтобы поиск по заголовку находил один раздел — в архитектуре.

### Вводная раздела 2

The points "To decide when detailed" of the card — a subsection for each point. Each is decided by a task of its own of epic E1 in [RUN.md](../../RUN.md). A decided point names the entry of the decision log, and the decision itself moves to section 3. A question that goes beyond this repository goes «в реестр spec» (RULES.md, Across repositories).

The card had four points for E1 — subsections 2.1–2.4, in its order. The fifth, the workbook, the card gave 2026-10-22 — it is subsection 2.7. Two of them rest on questions of `spec`'s registers: the docking and publishing of the package on Т-21 and Т-26, the format of the samples on the eval protocol (R15 spec) and the volunteers' consent (П-16). There the task prepares the answer from `canvas`'s side, and the decision itself is recorded in `spec`.

All four points of E1 are decided: 2.1 — R05, 2.2 — R06, 2.3 — R07 and R08, 2.4 — R09 and R11. How they are built is section 3, and the subsections stay here as a record of what was known and what was chosen from. "What is known" in them was written before the decision, so "still open" and "not decided" there speak of the day of writing, and the answer is in "Decided". Open in them is only the probe of the build from a file — T29 —, and the rest waits for others: the eval protocol and the format of the samples for epic E3 in `spec`'s RUN.md, the texts of the consent for epic E4 there too, and what is in a sample and how one consents to it for П-18. Т-21 and Т-26 are closed by `canvas`'s answers — R36 and R37 spec (R16).

Subsection 2.5 is beyond the card and not from epic E1: input on the text canvas. The author's analysis found it 2026-10-21 (sources 1.5), and epic E3 «Текстовая канва» was to decide it. The epic was dropped 2026-10-25: `canvas` builds no text canvas (R21). So 2.5 stays as a record, as does what 2.1–2.3 say about the text canvas.

Subsection 2.6 is beyond the card too: scrolling the sheet with one pointer. The planning of T35 found it (R29), and T45 decided it (R33) — with the buttons «Выше» and «Ниже». The accessibility level, which stayed open, was decided by the standard of UI packages R90 spec, and О-24 is solved; what of it the element holds and what it does not yet meet is section 3.10. Since T121 the buttons are gone: the sheet is moved by dragging, and 2.5.7 is a declared nonconformance (R84; the second 2026-11-14 amendment to R90 spec).

Subsection 2.7 is the workbook. It has been a point of the card since 2026-10-22, when it entered the first slice instead of the text canvas: the page, the answer slots, the slot's crop and the template layer (R41 spec; architecture 3.2 spec). It was decided by T46 (R34), and built by T47, T51, T48, T49 and T53 (R35–R39). How the workbook is built is section 3.8, where T50 brought it together, and the subsection, like 2.1–2.4, stays as a record of what was known and what was chosen from.

Subsection 2.8 is two points the card gave 2026-10-27, in `spec` v0.2.40 and v0.2.41: the task's new fields with the exit condition "there is something in each slot", and the demo of a worked example. They were decided by epic E7 (R40): T55 took the fields, T56 decided the rest (R42), and T57–T61 and T64 built it. How it is built is section 3.9, where T62 brought it together, and the subsection, like 2.7, stays as a record of what was known and what was chosen from.

### Подраздел 2.1. The drawing engine

- **Gist:** what the canvas draws the sheet and the layers with and takes the input of pen, finger and mouse with: a ready library — canvas-api planned Konva — or our own code on the browser's API. It is decided here: it is a point of the card (architecture 3.2 spec), and a component's technologies are decided by its repository (R11 spec).
- **What is known:**
  - what the drawing engine must be able to do the card and the vocabulary say: five layers, the tutor's sketch under the learner's writing, and the frames and marks above it; the task's tools; an eraser that removes a stroke whole; strokes with time and pressure; the picture of the work for the check; marks tied to the learner's strokes (architecture 3.2 spec; concepts 8 spec);
  - in the first slice there are two kinds — the handwriting sheet and the text canvas (product 10 spec). The text canvas is sentences and words: strike out, insert, reorder, highlight, listen (concepts 8 spec). Whether it is drawn by the same drawing engine as the sheet is not decided yet;
  - canvas-api planned Konva together with `react-konva`, the binding for React: in its spec 3.1, `konva` 10.7.x and `react-konva` 19.3.x for the layers of writing and hints. Its client `web/` still has neither — only React 19.3.0, so MathTrail has no actual experience with Konva (sources 1.1). Konva came there with the decision to keep the client in one repository with the API (R13 canvas-api), and there is no argument for Konva itself there; the argument about the repository was removed by R10 spec (repositories 1 spec);
  - standalone compared drawing libraries for the pictures of tasks in its widget — still drawings, not a handwriting sheet — and rejected Konva and the other libraries on `<canvas>`: such a drawing surface is one element, which a screen reader will not read, and a change of theme needs a redraw; `react-konva` moreover needs React (R269 standalone; sources 1.2). Accessibility is a point of `app`'s card (architecture 3.1 spec);
  - the prototype in `spec/draft/` draws the sheet straight on `<canvas>`, and its eraser erases pixels, not a whole stroke. Where the prototype differs from the documents, the documents are right (sources 1.3);
  - input in canvas-api is Pointer Events: `pointerType` tells the pen from the finger, `pressure` gives the pressure. The pressure changes only the line's width on screen and does not get into the picture for the check; a palm's touch is rejected (spec 3.1 canvas-api, sources 1.1);
  - how the package docks with the application is still open (Т-21; section 2.3). Т-21's recommendation is web components: the framework of each consumer is decided by its repository. So a drawing engine that holds on to the consumer's framework is checked against both answers to Т-21;
  - the layer of the module's widget is from stage 4, and the widget runs in an iframe sandbox (R16 spec; architecture 3.4 spec);
  - the dependencies are at exact versions and only with licenses compatible with Apache-2.0 (RULES.md, Hard rules; R12 spec);
  - the research «Рукопись ребёнка на канве и ИИ-наставник» is not a decision but an input of T01 and T02 (sources 1.4): for the check the canvas gives a crop — by A3 and H3; the group boxes of stroke groups — by A6 — go into the samples and into the tying of marks on the device; the canvas receives the marks of the tutor's layer and holds them to the strokes — by C2 (section 2.2);
  - tldraw is a whiteboard SDK under a license of its own. Without a trial or commercial key it requires "Not to use the Software in Production Environments". Other licenses tldraw gives separately — "Alternative licenses are available from tldraw for commercial and non-commercial use" —, and their terms were not read. And of the code itself the license says: "The Software may collect and transmit usage data to tldraw for license compliance purposes" (sources 2.1). The license of `LICENSE.md` is not ASF's category A, to which R12 spec limits what ships with the package, and the exception for development tools does not fit tldraw: it would ship inside `canvas`. The data collection also conflicts with `canvas` calling no one over the network (repositories 2.1 spec), and with the samples page going to no network while it runs (R25 spec). Its Agent starter kit is built on `tldraw`, goes to AI providers by itself and changes the shapes on the board (sources 2.2) — against R03, R05 and R15 spec. The research refers to tldraw's bindings by shape ID as a model, but its labels on strokes (C2) do not need tldraw;
  - the candidates the author named 2026-10-21 after the discussion are not a decision: Konva 10 for the scene and the layers, perfect-freehand 1.2 for the stroke's outline and rbush 4 for the index of strokes, all under MIT; input is Pointer Events (sources 2.3 and 3.1).
- **Options** — by the same criteria for all, from the sources (sources 2.1, 2.3, 2.4, 2.5, 2.6, 2.7 and 3.1), read 2026-10-21. The size is the build's main file from jsdelivr, compressed by `gzip -9`. "Both" in the column Т-21 means the option suits both a web component and a component of `app`'s framework.

  | Option | Version and license | Eraser by whole stroke | Crop off screen; Node | Accessibility | Pressure | Size | Т-21 |
  |---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
  | own code on the browser's API with perfect-freehand and rbush | perfect-freehand 1.2.3 — MIT; rbush 4.0.1 — MIT, with `quickselect` 3.0.0 — ISC | own: rbush finds the strokes by their bounding boxes, the exact distance is computed by our own code | own raster and own PNG; in Node, the same code | the statement and the tools are HTML, the marks SVG; on the drawing surface, only the ink | perfect-freehand's outline by `pressure` | ≈2.0 KB, and ≈2.3 KB with `quickselect` | both |
  | Konva | 10.9.0 — MIT | in Free Drawing, `destination-out`, that is, pixels | `toCanvas` and `toBlob`, by the browser's drawing surface; in Node, the package `canvas` or `skia-canvas` | "does not create accessible DOM elements"; HTML only on top of the scene | only from the native event `evt` | ≈58 KB | both |
  | Fabric.js | 7.4.0 — MIT | in `@erase2d/fabric` 1.2.2, `destination-out` and a clip path | by the drawing surface; in Node, node-canvas and jsdom | nothing | no | ≈92 KB | both |
  | Paper.js | 0.14.0 — MIT; 0.12.18 came out 2024-07-17, and the next release only 2026-10-18 | no; there is the blend `destination-out` | by the drawing surface; in Node, jsdom and node-canvas | nothing | no | ≈74 KB | both |
  | PixiJS | 8.22.0 — MIT | the blend `erase`, pixels | WebGL, WebGPU or the drawing surface; no ready adapter for Node | an overlay of `div` on top of the drawing surface, by choice | yes | ≈237 KB | both |
  | Two.js | 0.8.24 — MIT | no: there is no input at all | SVG, the drawing surface or WebGL; in Node, node-canvas | nothing | — | ≈50 KB | both |
  | Excalidraw | 0.18.1 — MIT | yes, the element whole | the drawing surface; it draws only in the browser | nothing | yes, through perfect-freehand | ≈155 KB — one `index.js`, without the chunks and 31 dependencies | only with React |
  | tldraw | 5.5.2 — a license of its own, not ASF's category A | — | — | — | — | — | only with React |
  | signature_pad, Atrament | 5.1.4 and 5.1.0 — MIT | `destination-out` | the drawing surface; Atrament throws an error outside the browser | nothing | yes; signature_pad's width comes from the speed | ≈4.7 and ≈2.6 KB | both |
  | drauu | 1.0.0 — MIT | yes, the SVG path whole | SVG; needs the DOM | nothing | yes, through perfect-freehand | ≈8.1 KB unminified | both |

  None of the options gives the tying of marks to strokes, the writing history and palm rejection: in any of them it is our own code. The ID bindings the research refers to as a model (C2) carry over without tldraw too. Every option builds into one file without the network, except tldraw: its code checks the license key and may transmit usage data. Inside our own code the ink can be drawn on SVG too, as drauu does. Theme and zoom are simpler then, but each stroke is a DOM element, and the low-latency mode `desynchronized` exists only for the drawing surface.
- **Decided** (R05): the drawing engine is our own code on the browser's API with perfect-freehand and rbush, and the text canvas is the page's markup in the same stack of layers. Stroke groups and the crop are computed by our own DOM-free code. How it is built: section 3.1.
- **Task:** T01 — done.

### Подраздел 2.2. The sheet's logical coordinates

- **Gist:** in what units the canvas keeps the strokes, draws the picture or the crop for the check and takes the tutor's marks — so that they do not depend on the screen —, what size the sheet and the picture are, and who converts into the sheet's units what the model returned. It is decided here: it is a point of the card (architecture 3.2 spec). But the picture's sizes and the units of the marks enter the contract `app` ↔ `api` and the observation — a type shared by `tutor`, `module-kit` and eval (repositories 5 spec). Their format — protobuf and buf — was decided by R11 spec, and the contracts themselves are written by epic E3 in `spec`'s RUN.md. So what the task decides about them goes «в реестр spec».
- **What is known:**
  - R15 canvas-api: the coordinates are kept in the page's logical system — it has a fixed width, the y axis points down, the scale is the same on both axes, and the normalization 0..1000 appears only at the boundary with Gemini. A normalization by the screen's width and height would distort the picture on screens of other proportions (sources 1.1);
  - spec 4 canvas-api: the sheet is 1000 × 1400 units, a cell 50 units; the picture for the check is the whole sheet without cropping, one unit is one pixel; on it are only the strokes — without the statement, the cells and the frames of hints; the sizes are constants of the protocol, and the server accepts only them. This section was to be rewritten by task T67 canvas-api, but it is not done (sources 1.1);
  - R43 canvas-api: the model returns the error's box in fractions 0..1000 of the picture, the server converts it into the sheet's units, and the client circles the strokes of which at least half the points lie in the box widened by 10 units. If there are no such strokes, there is no box, and only the question remains (sources 1.1). The point of the card "the tutor's marks, tied to the learner's strokes" rests on this (architecture 3.2 spec);
  - NovaTrail has several AI providers (R07 spec), and everything that calls a model is in `tutor` (architecture 1 spec). In what units the observation carries the box — in the sheet's units, and then `tutor`'s adapter converts it, or in fractions of the picture, and then `api` converts it — is not decided yet (repositories 5 spec; epic E3 in `spec`'s RUN.md);
  - a sheet in NovaTrail is squared, ruled or dotted (concepts 8 spec), and what goes to the check is a picture or a crop (architecture 3.2 spec; R09 spec);
  - the strokes with time and pressure stay on the device (R09 spec). In canvas-api they were also kept in logical units (R15 canvas-api, the 2026-10-11 amendment; sources 1.1);
  - the research «Рукопись ребёнка на канве и ИИ-наставник» advises (sources 1.4): the crop by A3, and the digit height a setting of the module's canvas; the group boxes of stroke groups in fractions 0–1000 of the crop, `[ymin, xmin, ymax, xmax]`, and the crop's place on the sheet, `sheet_box`, in the sheet's units; groups by the geometry of the final sheet, without time (C1). The place of an error is the answer slot, then the group label, then the model's box snapped to the strokes «точки которых в основном внутри», and the margin and the threshold are parameters of eval (C1–C2). canvas-api had a rule of its own — R43 canvas-api above;
  - the task of the module format v0 about the task's canvas — T28 in `spec`'s RUN.md at the tag v0.2.12 — takes this task as an input: the zone of an answer slot is set in the sheet's units (sources 5.3);
  - each AI provider names a place on a picture in its own way. Claude answers in pixels of the picture after its own downscaling, and works poorly with fractions 0–1000. Gemini gives `[ymin, xmin, ymax, xmax]` in fractions 0–1000. OpenAI has no box format, and it advises converting the coordinates back onto the original picture (sources 4.1);
  - `tutor` puts into the measurement an analysis with a box «в координатах своего поставщика», which the runner snaps to the strokes. The groups for labels it builds «по пространству и времени», and it draws the PNG with digits of 16, 24, 32 and 48 px (R08 tutor). How the observation points to a place and what the digits' height should be, `tutor` passed «в реестр spec»: these are В12 tutor and В13 tutor (sources 5.2).
- **Options** — from the sources (sources 1.1, 1.4 and 5.2):

  | Question | Options | Decided |
  |---|---|---|
  | the sheet | one sheet of 1000 × 1400 units, as in canvas-api: it is fit to the width, and what does not fit in the height scrolls; the same sheet whole on the screen; the height a setting of the module; several pages | the first |
  | the picture for the check | a crop by A3; the whole sheet of 1000 × 1400 px, one unit one pixel, as in canvas-api; a crop by the box of the work, but without scaling | the first |
  | the place from the model | fractions 0–1000 of the crop, and `canvas` converts them into the sheet's units; the sheet's units, converted by `tutor`'s adapter; fractions, and `api` converts them into the sheet's units | the first |
  | stroke groups | by space and time, as R08 tutor; by the geometry of the final sheet, without time (C1); the device and the runner each with rules of their own | the first |
  | the text canvas's marks | to words by ID; to offsets in characters; to the coordinates of the layout | the first |

  Why each was chosen and what is wrong with the others: R06.
- **Decided** (R06):
  - the sheet is 1000 × 1400 units, as in canvas-api;
  - what goes to the check is a crop by A3;
  - the place in the observation is in fractions 0–1000 of the crop, and `canvas` converts it into the sheet's units;
  - stroke groups are by space and time;
  - the text canvas's marks are tied to words by ID.

  How it is built: section 3.2.
- **Task:** T02 — done.

### Подраздел 2.3. Docking with the application and publishing the package (Т-21, Т-26)

- **Gist:** by what interface the package enters `app` and other builds — a web component or a component of `app`'s framework —, what types and events this interface has, and in what registry its releases come out, so that `app` installs it at an exact version. This is a point of the card (architecture 3.2 spec), but both questions are in `spec`'s register: Т-21 concerns all UI packages and their consumers (architecture 6 spec), Т-26 also `skill-map` (architecture 3.3 spec). Here the task prepares the answer from `canvas`'s side, and the decision goes «в реестр spec» (RULES.md, Across repositories).
- **What is known:**
  - `canvas` and `skill-map` live in repositories of their own, with releases of their own, and `app` builds them in at an exact version. Rejected: loading at run time — web components by URL, Module Federation — and packages inside the repository `app` (R16 spec);
  - during development `app` uses working copies of the packages, and only exact versions go into a release. The second consumer — a site, the card of the MCP channel — gets a second build of the same package, like the chat widget in standalone (architecture 6 spec);
  - Т-21's recommendation is web components with types from `spec`: the packages will have several consumers — `app`, the samples page, a site, the card of the MCP channel —, and the framework of each is decided by its repository. `app`'s framework is not chosen yet: in canvas-api, React 19 and Vite 8 (R47 canvas-api), in standalone, Preact (architecture 3.1 spec);
  - the component's code will take the types from the generated packages of `spec`'s releases (R21 spec; R36 spec; R45 spec). What types the canvas's interface will need — the task's open view, the strokes, the picture for the check, the tutor's marks — is not named yet;
  - the options of Т-26 are npm in the scope `@altnovatrail`, GitHub Packages, only GitHub releases. Its recommendation is to decide after reading the registries' terms: the packages must install at an exact version and without a token, by widget authors too. By GitHub's documentation, an npm package from GitHub Packages cannot be installed without a token, even a public one (sources 7.1);
  - from stage 4 the canvas is also the application's side of the widget protocol (architecture 3.2 spec; repositories 3 spec). The protocol itself is R16 spec and Т-24, epic E5 in `spec`'s RUN.md, and what it has to decide beyond R16 spec is named by architecture 3.4 spec (section 3.1);
  - the release of `canvas` will also include the build of the samples page (R25 spec) and synthetic samples for `tutor`'s CI (R38 spec);
  - the shared TypeScript wrapper of `.github` is epic E5 in `.github`'s RUN.md. It was expected with the first TypeScript code, `canvas`'s (R03 .github, the 2026-10-21 amendment), but it came out earlier — in v0.1.5, after a probe in `.github` itself. Its agreement with `canvas`'s recipes is R19 .github, with the amendments to it (section 3.4; sources 5.4);
  - `app`'s framework is not chosen yet — its T01 is open —, and both `app` and `skill-map` wait for the answer to Т-21. `app` also put off "listen" on the text canvas until Т-21: «один ли у них с `app` синтез речи, решается вместе со стыковкой пакетов (Т-21)» (sources 5.1);
  - React 19 and Preact take a custom element themselves: they pass values by its properties if the element has declared them, and listen to events by name (sources 3.3);
  - ARIA references by ID do not cross the boundary of the Shadow DOM, and a custom element's name is one per page (sources 3.3);
  - npm: anyone can install a public package in a scope, a published version does not change, and an organization for public packages is free. Trusted publishing allows publishing without a long-lived token, and from a reusable workflow npm checks the name of the calling workflow. It can be set up only for a package that is already in the registry (sources 7.2);
  - in npm 12 installing by a link is closed by default: `allow-remote` is `none`, and the exception is only archives from the registry's own host. In npm 11, which comes with Node 24, it is still open (sources 7.2 and 7.3);
  - GitHub Packages requires a token to install a public package too, and only a personal access token (classic) (sources 7.1);
  - `.github`'s shared release is `release.yml`, which came out in v0.1.4 (R18 .github). The files are written by the caller's recipe `release-files`, and each is signed by cosign without keys. The first release on it is `module-kit` v0.1.0 of 2026-10-21. The shared release does not publish to npm, and what a consumer checks of its signature is decided by T37 `spec` (Т-27; sources 5.4 and 5.5);
  - T32 `tutor` waits for the answer where in `canvas`'s release the synthetic samples are, and T04 `skill-map` takes `canvas`'s conclusions about Т-26 (sources 5.1 and 5.2).
- **Options** of the docking — from the sources (sources 3.3 and 5.1):

  | Question | Options | Decided |
  |---|---|---|
  | what the package enters the consumer as (Т-21) | a web component without a framework; a component of `app`'s framework; a mount function | the first |
  | how many elements | one for all canvas kinds; one of its own for each kind | the first |
  | where the element's markup is | an open Shadow DOM; the page's shared DOM | the first |
  | who voices "listen" | `app`, on the canvas's event; the canvas itself | the first |

  Why each was chosen and what is wrong with the others: R07.
- **Options** of publishing — from the sources (sources 5.4, 7.1 and 7.2):

  | Question | Options | Decided |
  |---|---|---|
  | where the package comes out (Т-26) | npm, the scope `@altnovatrail`; GitHub Packages; only GitHub releases | the first |
  | how the package gets to npm | a step of the shared `release.yml` — the same file as in the signed release; a job of its own in `canvas`'s `release.yml`; staged publishing; by hand, each version | the first |
  | where the samples page's build and the synthetic samples are | files of the same GitHub release; npm packages; separate releases | the first |

  Why each was chosen and what is wrong with the others: R08.
- **Decided:**
  - the docking (R07) is the web component `<novatrail-canvas>`, one for all canvas kinds and with an open Shadow DOM. It receives data by properties, gives the work for the check by a method, and the writing history and the request to voice by events. How it is built: section 3.3;
  - publishing and the release (R08): the package comes out on npm, `@altnovatrail/canvas`, and everything `canvas` puts out is the files of one GitHub release with the signature of `.github`'s shared `release.yml`. The same package file goes to npm, by a step of the same release. How it is built: section 3.4.
- **Tasks:** T03 — the docking, done; T27 — publishing and the release, done.

### Подраздел 2.4. The format of the samples by the eval protocol

- **Gist:** what the samples page writes into the sample file — the picture of the sheet, the strokes, the task, what the volunteer meant to write — so that `tutor`'s eval runner reads the sample by the eval protocol, and the file holds nothing extra about the volunteer. This is a point of the card (architecture 3.2 spec), but the eval protocol is set by `spec` (R15 spec), and the eval protocol and the format of the samples are epic E3 in `spec`'s RUN.md. So the task prepares what the canvas and the samples page need, and what belongs to the format goes «в реестр spec».
- **What is known:**
  - the eval protocol is in `spec`, the eval runner in `tutor`, learners' work outside the repositories (R15 spec). A module's eval set is synthetic samples and samples that adult volunteers and authors made for eval (concepts 3 spec; R02 spec);
  - a sample is made with the same canvas as the learners have, and is saved as a file on the device; an adult volunteer's sample gets into the eval set by a pull request to `modules`; nothing goes to the platform's server (architecture 3.2 spec). The path of the pull request and the checks before the merge are decided by task T02 modules;
  - the samples are needed for the measurement of stage 2 — AI reading for each canvas kind and answer, including on handwriting that imitates a child's. Children's samples only with a separate consent and outside the repositories (repositories 6 spec; П-10). The measurement's main metric is false alarms (product 10 spec);
  - the samples in a module are made by adults: a module is published under an open license, and it cannot be revoked (concepts 3 spec). How the samples page, which anyone can open, keeps to this if children's samples are made with it too, but without a pull request (R25 spec), is not decided yet; how to confirm in the platform that a person is an adult is open question П-6;
  - the volunteers' consent is open question П-16. Its recommendation is a written consent to publication under the module's license, with a warning that it cannot be revoked, and neither names nor signatures on the samples; the text of the consent is decided by the author on Claude's analysis together with П-1 (R112 spec). П-16 is decided by epic E4 in `spec`'s RUN.md. Volunteers' samples in the eval sets of the official modules are published under their license, Apache-2.0 (R20 spec); what licenses third-party modules have is О-10. What follows from the recommendation of П-16 for the samples page — what it asks the volunteer and what it writes into the file — is decided by the task, not by this section;
  - what goes to the check is a picture or a crop, and the strokes with time and pressure stay on the device (R09 spec). A sample for eval may carry more: the author's log in canvas-api also kept the strokes as they are — points with time, the pointer type, pressure and tilt — for the research «картинка или штрихи» (R66 canvas-api), and the author put the markup before the submission, so that it would not adjust to the model's answer (R65 canvas-api). The research — task T77 canvas-api — is not done (sources 1.1);
  - the samples page for the first measurement is without hosting: the build is downloaded from the release of `canvas` and opened on the volunteer's device. By R25 spec no one's logs will see the volunteer's IP address, and they open the pull request on GitHub anyway; the download from the release itself is seen by GitHub too. Whether the build opened from a file works R25 spec tells T04 to check, and the probe T29 does it (R09). The page will move to the hosting of `app`'s build when there is one; the hosting is chosen by `infra` by what it writes to logs (R25 spec; Т-17; П-9). A child's sample has no pull request, and the build for it is downloaded from the release of `canvas` by the adult who runs the session, like any visitor of GitHub: GitHub sees their IP address, but the build's file holds neither a sample nor the child's data, and the sample file goes nowhere this way. The text of the separate consent to children's samples says so, and it is finally decided together with П-10 and П-12 (the 2026-10-20 amendment to R25 spec; П-10);
  - children's samples are needed before the beta, but only with a separate written consent and outside the repositories (product 10 spec; П-10). They are made with the same canvas and the same samples page, but without a pull request; where the file goes and who has access to it is decided by П-10 and П-12 — epic E4 in `spec`'s RUN.md (R25 spec). Children's samples do not go to an AI provider until П-3 has given a contract that admits children (R24 spec);
  - a sample is made for a module's task, and the canvas knows neither `api` nor subjects: the open view is built by `module-kit`, the media files in the first slice are served by `api` (architecture 1, 3.10 spec). The tasks and media files are put into the page's build by its CI — from the release of `modules` by the CLI of `module-kit`, by exact tag —, and while it runs the page goes to no network. Whether the CLI needs a command of its own for open views is decided by the detailing of `module-kit` (R25 spec; architecture 3.7 spec);
  - `tutor` is already running the measurement of AI reading, beside its detailing (R04 tutor). The samples for it, by R05 tutor, will be written by its recording page, while `canvas` has no samples page: the whole writing history — each stroke as points with time and pressure, in the order of writing, and each erasure with its time (R07 tutor). `tutor` proposes the format of its sample as the base of the eval protocol v0, and T04 is to check the samples page's sample against it (В7 tutor). The recording page itself does not exist yet: what it writes T08 `tutor` specifies, and as of 2026-10-21 that task is not done (sources 5.2). The format of `tutor`'s sample is so far described only in text: T12 tutor writes it, the recording page T13 tutor, and neither is done. The markup in it is by lines, «задуманный текст каждой строки и её штрихи», and the groups and pictures are drawn by the runner (sources 5.2);
  - the research «Рукопись ребёнка на канве и ИИ-наставник» (sources 1.4): the writing history is a condition of eval only, and raw pen dynamics are the riskiest legal class (A4, A6). By the plan of E2, which the author approved 2026-10-21, the writing history is provisionally only in adults' samples and in the synthetic samples, and a single crop goes into the check's flow (R09 spec). The file's composition was finally decided by T04 — R09, below;
  - where the samples page takes the measurement's tasks from while `modules` and `module-kit` have no code is В6 in table 3 of [RUN.md](../../RUN.md), «в реестр spec». The measurement's tasks are now in `tutor`, with the key, the solution, the traps and the leak phrases (architecture 3.1 tutor).
- **Options** — from the sources (sources 1.1, 1.4 and 5.2):

  | Question | Options | Decided |
  |---|---|---|
  | what sets the file's composition | three fields — the purpose, who wrote, the provenance; one field "kind of sample" | the first |
  | who has the writing history | only an adult's measurement sample, and the synthetic samples in every composition; all adults' samples and the synthetic samples, as the plan of E2; all, the child's too | the first |
  | what instead of the history in the eval-set samples and the child's | the final geometry of the visible strokes, with crops and groups; only crops and group boxes | the first |
  | groups for a child | by space and time, as in the work; without time | the first |
  | who draws the crops at the eval heights | `canvas` on saving, and the runner the states; one crop, and the rest the runner; the runner draws everything | the first |
  | the writer code | in the measurement's and the child's samples; no code, and lists of IDs outside the files keep the link; in all samples | the first |
  | when the markup is put | the intended text before writing the line, the rest before saving; all after the sheet | the first |

  Why each was chosen and what is wrong with the others: R09.
- **Decided** (R09): the file's composition is set by the purpose, who wrote and the provenance. The writing history is only in an adult's measurement sample, and in the eval-set samples and the child's, the final geometry of the visible strokes; the synthetic samples are in every composition. In all of them: crops at the eval heights, groups, markup by lines and a link to the task without the key. What is proposed to the protocol `spec` recorded in epic E3 of its RUN.md, and what the author decides on Claude's analysis, in П-18 (R16; R112 spec). How it is built: section 3.5. The page's steps (R11): the purpose is chosen on the page; the consent is a notice on the page and a signed form outside it; while the texts are a draft, only the author's measurement is open; the age is the declaration «18 лет или больше»; the writer code is one per person. How it is built: section 3.6. Where the measurement's tasks come from while `modules` has no first content `spec` decided by В6: from the release of `tutor`, by the file of open views (the 2026-10-21 amendment to R25 spec; R16).
- **Tasks:** T04 — the file's composition, done; T28 — the page's steps: age, consent, the child sample session, done; T29 — the probe of the build from a file on a computer, put off together with the big measurement (R17), brought back with epic E5 2026-11-02 and done 2026-11-08: in Chrome and Firefox on Linux the build from a file works (R52; measurements 1.1). Tablets are in emulators (the 2026-11-02 amendment to R17): Android — T74, done 2026-11-08 —, Chrome opens the downloaded file as `content://`, and the page works (R54; measurements 1.2); iPad — T80, done 2026-11-08: "Files" opens the file in Quick Look, and the script runs neither on iPadOS 26.5 nor on 27 (measurements 1.3; В64 in table 3 of [RUN.md](../../RUN.md)).

### Подраздел 2.5. Input on the text canvas — as typed

- **Gist:** the text canvas gives to the check what the learner typed. But the browser and the device's keyboard change what was typed by themselves: they correct words, put capital letters, suggest continuations and mark errors. Then the check kind "text" or "edit" would compare with the key not the learner's answer but the device's correction — for example, a stress mark that autocorrect put where the module counts stress marks as significant (concepts 9.2, 16.2 spec). And a marked error is a hint even before the check, that "red pen" the platform does without (product 7 spec). The card has no such point: the author's analysis found it 2026-10-21 (sources 1.5) — «нормализация, автокоррекция и „угадывание“ нужного слова в канве запрещены».
- **What is known:**
  - HTML Standard (sources 3.5): with `autocorrect="off"` the browser may not correct words while typing, and without the attribute `on` applies; with `spellcheck="false"` spelling is not checked, and with the check on the browser marks errors — exactly how, the standard does not set; with `writingsuggestions="false"` the browser does not suggest continuations;
  - the attributes are a request, not a guarantee. A check the user has turned on always `spellcheck="false"` does not turn off, and `autocapitalize` the browser or the keyboard "may choose to ignore or override", and the standard does not tell one to rely on it (sources 3.5). How the keyboards of iPad and Android behave is seen only on devices — the same way T22 checks the pen;
  - turning off has a price — accessibility (concepts 15 spec): for a learner with motor impairments or with dyslexia, autocorrect and suggestions can help in typing. Accessibility is a point of `app`'s card (architecture 3.1 spec);
  - normalization is a parameter of the check kind, and the module sets it: case, spaces, punctuation, stress marks (concepts 9.2 spec). The canvas has nothing to normalize;
  - this subsection's recommendation is taken as an input by T29 in `spec`'s RUN.md — the check kinds and the normalization of the module format v0 — with the same argument: otherwise normalization would check not the learner's answer but the device's correction;
  - input on the text canvas is "a keyboard with stress marks" (section 3.1). Whether it is a panel of the canvas's own or the device's keyboard is decided by the epic of the text canvas.
- **Options:** turn everything off — `autocorrect="off"`, `autocapitalize="none"`, `spellcheck="false"`, `writingsuggestions="false"` — and check the keyboards on devices; leave it as set on the device; let the module decide for each task.
- **Recommendation:** turn everything off in the answer field and check on devices: no check needs autocorrect, so the module needs no setting either. What to replace the typing help with for whoever needs it is to be decided in the same epic, together with `app`'s accessibility. There too: what to do if the device's keyboard does not obey the attributes — for example, an on-screen keyboard of the canvas's own.
- **Task:** epic E3 «Текстовая канва», which T05 proposed. It was dropped 2026-10-25 (R21): `canvas` builds no text canvas, and the subsection is not decided. The subsection's finding concerns any typed answer — the answer slots with keyboard input too, which `app` shows (concepts 6 spec). The «Вход» of T29 in `spec`'s RUN.md refers to the subsection — in case the text canvas enters format v0. It is not in the first slice (R41 spec), and В32 is closed so (R30).

### Подраздел 2.6. Scrolling the sheet with one pointer

- **Gist:** the drawing surface covers the visible part of the sheet, and it has `touch-action: none`: the browser does not take a touch that writes for scrolling (R18). Since T35 two fingers scroll the sheet on the drawing surface, by our own code (R29). WCAG 2.2 requires that what is done by a multipoint or path-based gesture be done by a single pointer without a path too (2.5.1, level A), and what is done by dragging, by a single pointer without it (2.5.7, AA). By Understanding 2.5.7 the criterion "does apply if content actively suppresses the user agent's own scrolling functionality and/or implements its own scrolling mechanism", and the keyboard alone is not enough. The card has no such point: the planning of T35 found it (sources 9.10).
- **What is known:**
  - on a screen no wider than 1000 CSS px — on a tablet — the drawing surface covers the sheet's whole width. On an element with a height the sheet then cannot be scrolled with one finger: the row of buttons scrolls the page, not the container, and the statement goes up together with the sheet;
  - on an element without a height the sheet scrolls together with the page, and a finger scrolls the page on the statement, on the row and outside the element by the browser's native scrolling. The criteria do not apply to it: 2.5.7 "does not apply to scrolling and dragging gestures enabled by the user agent";
  - the scrollbar on a tablet stands on top of the content, and a tablet usually has no wheel and no keyboard;
  - what WCAG level is required of UI packages `spec` has not decided — it is О-24. The shared accessibility standard of UI packages will be passed «в реестр spec» by T07 `app`, and the row of buttons and `canvas`'s table of labels are its input (the first 2026-10-26 amendment to R36 spec);
  - by `spec`'s addition to О-24, scrolling of our own by two fingers falls under 2.5.1 of level A too: Understanding 2.5.1 — "This success criterion applies to gestures in the author-provided content". Dragging with one finger would also do as the replacement at level A — 2.5.1 "does not apply to gestures that involve dragging in any direction" —, but on the sheet the finger writes, and in pen mode it does nothing (R29). So on an element with a height on a narrow tablet, without one of the options below, the sheet does not meet even level A.
- **Options:**
  - the buttons «Выше» and «Ниже» in the element's row, which scroll by the visible part with the same scrolling as two fingers (R29);
  - `app` gives the element no height on devices with touch, and the sheet scrolls together with the page;
  - an exception for the sheet in the accessibility standard.
- **Recommendation:** the buttons «Выше» and «Ниже», if the standard requires level AA — so `canvas` advised in В41, and so it is written in О-24. By the addition to О-24 they are needed at level A too, while `app` gives the element a height on devices with touch: two buttons and two labels, and the scrolling is already there. To be decided together with the standard T07 `app`.
- **Task:** open — О-24 in `spec` (R31), decided together with the accessibility standard T07 `app`. R29 refers to the subsection. Added 2026-10-26: by the author's decision in the planning of E6, the buttons «Выше» and «Ниже» are built by T45 in [RUN.md](../../RUN.md), for the sheet and the workbook, without waiting for the standard.
- **Decided** (R33): the buttons «Выше» and «Ниже» in the element's row, on any device. A press scrolls what two fingers would scroll, by ⅘ of what the scrolled container shows, and at the edge the button fades and keeps the focus. On a computer the buttons let the keyboard scroll an element with a height in Safari too, where a scroll container without focusable content cannot be scrolled from the keyboard (sources 9.11). The accessibility level still waits for the standard T07 `app` (О-24), and `canvas`'s answer to О-24 is В44 in table 3 of [RUN.md](../../RUN.md). How it is built: section 3.3. T45 — done. Added 2026-11-11: the keyboard scrolls the element's container itself too — a region with `tabindex="0"`, while its content overflows it, in Safari too (T93; the T93 amendment to R33; section 3.1). The buttons stay for a single pointer. Added 2026-11-17: T121 took the four buttons away by the second 2026-11-14 amendment to R90 spec — the sheet is dragged by one finger in pen mode, by two always, and scrolled by the wheel and the keys; 2.5.1 holds, since it "does not apply to gestures that involve dragging in any direction", and 2.5.7 is declared in section 3.10 (R84).

### Подраздел 2.7. The workbook

- **Gist:** how the canvas shows a task of the kind `workbook` and what it gives from it to the check. The format sets only the item line — pieces of text and boxes in order, at least one box —, and leaves the layout to the canvas: "How the lines are laid out on the page is the canvas's" (the format's README, §14; R51 spec). Since 2026-10-22 the card gives `canvas` the detailing of the workbook: the page, the answer slots, the slot's crop and the template layer (architecture 3.2 spec; R41 spec). It is decided here. And what concerns the agreement `app` ↔ `api`, the eval protocol, the agreement of `canvas` and `app` and the card itself goes «в реестр spec» (RULES.md, Across repositories).
- **What is known:**
  - the format (the format's README, §11 and §14; `task.schema.json`; R51 spec):
    - the workbook is "a page of lines, one for each item, that the learner does not erase, with a box in a line for each answer". An item is a line, `line`: pieces of text and boxes `binding: box` in order, and a box anywhere — "7 × [ ] = 56", "56 [ ] 65", "[ ] apples". The boxes of a line are its answer slots, and a slot is named by its place among the item's boxes, from 1: items and slots have no IDs;
    - the workbook has no grid: the schema rejects `grid` and `grid_step` for it;
    - there is no limit: `items` has no `maxItems`, and a piece of text only `minLength: 1`, so a space is a piece too;
    - every task has the pen and the eraser. Keyboard input into a box, a printed stacked fraction and column arithmetic will come later (T38 in `spec`'s RUN.md);
    - a piece of text is in the module's explanation language, like the statement, and the digit height is the manifest's `canvas.digit_height`. The format's README, §11, says of it — "on the crop of a handwriting sheet";
  - concepts spec:
    - each item has its own region on the canvas, its own key, its own skills and its own state — not started, started, checked —, and the task's result is made up of the items (concepts 6 spec);
    - the exit condition of a step is, among others, "a filled answer slot" (concepts 7 spec);
    - the workbook is "items and answer slots that cannot be erased, on one page", its example "ten examples on fractions". So far only the sheet shows the statement above the canvas (concepts 8 spec);
    - an observation is per slot, tied to the strokes or to a place on the canvas (concepts 9.1 spec), and the check kind "fraction and form" checks the notation of the fraction too (concepts 9.2 spec);
  - the agreement and the place:
    - R36 spec with its second amendment knows one crop — `exportWork()` with `{ png, submission }` — and the sheet's context: the crop's numbers, the groups with the table of group labels, and the digest. It is also named by privacy 4 spec;
    - R53 spec: the place in fractions 0–1000 of the crop, and "the zone of an answer slot, if it has to be shown to reading: `canvas` converts the zone, and the crop stays one". This is about the sheet's zones;
    - the crop of a workbook's slot waited for the "Revisit" of R25, and the card names it;
    - a mark names the slot by ID (R26), and slots have only a place;
  - how the sheet is built:
    - the sheet of 1000 × 1400 is a constant of the kind (R06), and the statement stands above it, in HTML: the place of text on the sheet would depend on the device's fonts, and the strokes lie in the sheet's units (R23). The statement's pictures have their size in CSS px (R51 spec);
    - `SHEET` holds the check of an assigned history, the crop, the marks and the screen (section 3.7). A history does not know its task, and `task` and `history` are assigned in any order (R27): what a history is checked by does not depend on the task;
    - the groups of R24 join strokes closer than 50 units and 2 s to each other, and the boxes of "47 ÷ 6 = [ ] r [ ]" stand closer. Group boxes at the check are Т-31;
  - `tutor` (sources 9.12): the measurement of the workbook is T78–T80 `tutor`, on synthetic samples, about 20 pages of 10 items. They are not started, and T46 does not wait for them. T79 `tutor` reads the slots «один вызов на страницу: вырезки ячеек с подписями — с текстом пунктов или без него», and T80 `tutor` compares reading with the item's text and without it. To the product `tutor` advises reading the workbook's slots on the device with a small digit network of its own, with a fallback to AI — that network needs the picture of a slot;
  - the prototype `spec/draft/` (sources 9.12) is an illustration, not the format. The statement is in a header above the page, and on the page are ten numbered items, in two columns. The box after "=" is 76 × 62 px with a dashed line below, an item has a marker «верно» of its own, and the tutor's mark is a dashed circle around the box;
  - fonts (sources 9.12): devices have different ones, and text that the browser lays out would lie differently on different devices. SVG fits text to a given length — `textLength`, and `lengthAdjust="spacingAndGlyphs"` compresses or stretches the glyphs themselves too. Both attributes are in all browsers. But WebKit lays out `textLength` with lists of x and y, `spacingAndGlyphs` and the collapsing of spaces otherwise than Chrome and Firefox: the fix was merged 2026-10-16 behind a flag off by default. Its methods of querying text gave the numbers before the stretch, and the fix of this was merged 2026-09-19. Whether both fixes came out in Safari is not checked;
  - accessibility (sources 9.12), by WCAG 2.2:
    - the reading order is programmatically determined (1.3.2);
    - a border without which the content cannot be understood has a contrast of at least 3:1 (1.4.11), and the sheet's grid, `#d3dbe5` on white, is about 1.4:1;
    - reflow on a narrow screen is not required where a two-dimensional layout is needed (1.4.10);
    - text must grow to 200 % without loss (1.4.4), and the sheet is fit to the width, and `k` is at most 1 (R23). Decided by T94: the buttons «Крупнее» and «Мельче» grow the sheet up to 200 % of its fit, with scrolling sideways (R73; section 3.2).

    Chromium reads an SVG with a list, its items, text and `role="img"` in order (a probe, sources 9.12). So Chromium's own accessibility tree reads it too — in the probes of T47 and T51 and in the element's test (R36). The accessibility level waits for О-24.
- **Options** — from the sources (sources 9.12):

  | Question | Options | Chosen |
  |---|---|---|
  | the page | 1000 × 1400, like the sheet; width 1000, height by the lines; the height a setting of the module | the first |
  | the statement | above the page, HTML, as on the sheet; on the page, in its units | the first |
  | a task that does not fit | `TypeError` on `task.items`, and a limit for authors «в реестр spec»; compress the text and the gaps down to a limit, and below it `TypeError`; compress everything without a limit | the first |
  | the lines with any font | a table of widths of our own in the DOM-free part, and `textLength`; a font of our own in the package; layout by the device's font | the first |
  | the box | one per task, with a border of 3:1; by the answer; a box that grows when one writes into it | the first |
  | the boxes' strokes | each stroke in one box or in none, by a rule in integers; by the stroke bundle; ink only inside the border | the first |
  | writing outside the boxes | a draft: on the page and in the history, but not at the check; not written at all; goes to the check as a crop of the page | the first |
  | groups | boxes instead of groups; R24 over the whole page; R24 inside the boxes and in the draft | the first |
  | filled boxes | the property `filled`; an event of its own; in `detail` of `historychange` | the first |
  | to the check | a crop of each filled box; one crop of the strokes of all boxes, and the boxes as frames in its fractions; a crop of the whole page, with the draft | the first |
  | the lines' accessibility | the template's SVG with a list and boxes `role="img"`; a hidden copy of the lines in HTML | the first |
  | the workbook sample | the boxes' crops, the device's table and the boxes' rectangles; everything as for the sheet (R09); decide in E5 | the first |

  Why each was chosen and what is wrong with the others: R34.
- **Recommendation** — the author accepted it in the planning of T46, 2026-10-27 (R34):
  - **the page** is 1000 × 1400 units, like the sheet, without a grid, and the statement is above it, as on the sheet. The items are lines in order, with numbers on the left: short ones in two columns, a long line wraps. The free space is the draft;
  - **the layout** is a function of one task, in the DOM-free part and in integers, and the glyphs' widths are a table of our own by classes. The text is drawn by the SVG of the template layer: a piece as one `<text>` at one point, fitted to its width by `textLength` and `spacingAndGlyphs`. The font does not move the boxes;
  - **a task that does not fit** is a `TypeError` on `task.items`, like a zone (R32): the boxes and the text are always full size. A limit for authors, with the layout's numbers, T47 will pass «в реестр spec»;
  - **the box** is one for the whole task, and holds a stacked fraction, a three-digit number and a sign. The border is thin, solid, with a contrast of at least 3:1, and the line's characters stand at the middle of the box's height. The ink is not clipped by the border;
  - **the boxes' strokes**: each visible stroke in one box or in none, by a rule in integers, without the DOM and without time:
    - the rule looks at each stroke separately and settles a disputed case itself — more points, then the earlier place;
    - its parameters are in fractions of the box;
    - the rest is the draft: it is on the page and in the history, but does not go to the check;
  - **there are no groups:** in the submission context, the table "box → stroke IDs" and the digest. A mark comes with an answer slot — the numbers of the item and of the box, from 1 —, and holds on to the strokes of its box by the rules of R26;
  - **`filled`** is a read-only property: per item, per box, whether it is filled, and on the sheet `null`. A box is filled if it has at least one stroke of those that would go into `exportWork()`. This is seen on the box itself too, not only by color;
  - **to the check** goes the whole page at once — as a crop of each filled box: the rule of R25 by its strokes, at the manifest's digit height, only the ink. An empty box is not read, the draft does not leave the device, and the place in the observation is the answer slot;
  - **accessibility**: the lines are read by a screen reader, in order. The template layer's SVG is a list of items, a box is `role="img"` with a name and whether it is filled, and the lines' language comes from the manifest;
  - **the workbook sample**: the boxes' crops at the eval heights, the table of boxes that the device built, and the boxes' rectangles in the page's units. In the eval-set samples and the child's, only the boxes' strokes.

  The rows «в реестр spec» are В45–В48 in table 3 of [RUN.md](../../RUN.md).
- **Decided** (R34): by the recommendation above. The layout's numbers are R35, the template layer R36, the rule of boxes and `filled` R37, the work for the check R38, the marks on boxes R39. How it is built: section 3.8.
- **Task:** T46 — decided in its planning (R34). Built by T47 — the page's layout (R35), T51 — the template layer (R36), T48 — the boxes' strokes and `filled` (R37), T49 — the work for the check (R38) and T53 — the marks on boxes (R39), all done. T47 and T51, like T49 and T53, are one task of the plan, which the author split in its planning. T50 brought how it is built together into section 3.

### Подраздел 2.8. The task's fields, filled slots and the example's demo

- **Gist:** what the element does with what the card gave `canvas` in `spec` v0.2.40 and v0.2.41 (architecture 3.2 spec, "To decide when detailed", the additions of 2026-10-27): with the task's new open fields, with the step's exit condition "there is something in every slot" and with the example's demo on the demo layer. They are decided together: all of it is the agreement between `canvas` and `app`.
- **What is known** (R40; `spec` v0.4.1, read 2026-11-01; sources 9.14):
  - a task has the open fields "givens and unknown": `givens`, a list of short texts, and `unknown`, a text, in the module's explanation language (R57 spec). They are chips that the learner marks at a solving step, and `app` shows them. Since T55 the element takes and checks them, and does not show them (the 2026-11-01 amendment to R32);
  - the exit condition `answer_slots_filled` is that every answer slot has something in it: "a stroke in its zone or its box, an option tapped, and for an answer slot bound `anywhere` or `sheet`, a stroke on the sheet" (the format's README, §18; R57 spec). On the workbook this is `filled` (R37). On the sheet `filled` is `null`, and what it is for `anywhere` and `sheet` "is still decided by `app` and `canvas`" (the 2026-10-27 amendment to R36 spec; architecture 3.1 spec). The same amendment rejected filledness in the `detail` of `historychange` and as an event of its own;
  - a task on the sheet has either one slot `anywhere` or `sheet`, the only one in the task (the format's README, §14; `maxItems` in the schema), or `choice` slots, whose options `app` shows and marks. A slot in a zone the element rejects for now (R32). `exportWork()` on the sheet gives `null` exactly when the sheet has no stroke, and that is when «Очистить» is unavailable too (R27, R28);
  - a worked example is not a task: no slots, no key. Its canvas is a sheet with a grid, and "The learner does not write on the sheet: it holds the author's demo and no learner's work". The demo's parts are a paragraph, a picture beside the sheet with a description and a size, or a picture on the sheet with a description and an area. "The learner opens them one at a time, and MAY hide the whole demo and show it again" (the format's README, §21; R58 spec). Its type is `WorkedExample` of the package `spec` (R41);
  - "Whether the paragraphs and the pictures beside the sheet stand above it or beside it is the canvas's to say, as for a task's statement" (ibid.). A task's statement on the sheet stands above it (R23);
  - the cards of `spec` give opening the parts and hiding the demo to both: `app` "the author's demo by parts and its hiding" in the lesson player, `canvas` "open a part and hide the whole demo" on the demo layer (architecture 3.1 and 3.2 spec; Д39 in RUN.md of `spec`). The lesson and the player of segments are `app`'s, and the element takes one segment, and each segment gets a new element (R20; «Решено автором» of epic E7);
  - the element already has an empty demo layer `show` between the widget's place and the tutor's sketch, `aria-hidden` (R23). Scrolling does not move the HTML layers: only the SVG ones change their `viewBox`. The element has no live regions for announcements, and the Tab stop in the row of buttons holds to «Перо» (R28).
- **Options:**

  | Question | Options | What is wrong with the rejected ones |
  |---|---|---|
  | `filled` on the sheet | 1) for an `anywhere` or `sheet` slot, `[[has a stroke]]`, otherwise `null`; 2) per slot, `null` for `choice`; 3) nothing new: `app` folds the history with `replay` | 2 changes the type for a shape that the format does not allow; 3: the condition would be asked not of the element, and `app` would repeat the eraser's rule until lift-off |
  | how the element takes an example | 1) a property of its own, `workedExample`; 2) the example through `task`; 3) an element of its own | 2 mixes the types and the errors of the check; 3: a second tag, a second registration and a second copy of the row of buttons |
  | who opens the parts | 1) the element's buttons, and for `app` a property and an event; 2) only `app`, by a property; 3) only the element, with no property | 2: `app`'s player does not exist yet, and there is nothing to try the example with; 3: `app` would not restore how many are open, and would not learn that the example was watched to the end |
  | where the parts beside the sheet go | 1) above the sheet, under the statement; 2) beside it on a wide screen, above it on a narrow one | 2: two layouts, an element wider than 1000 CSS px, and on a narrow tablet above the sheet anyway |
  | announcing an opened part | 1) a polite `status` region, the focus stays on the button; 2) the focus on the opened part | 2 breaks repeated presses of «Дальше» and leads out of the row of buttons |

- **Recommendation:** the first option for each question.
- **Decided** (R42; the author's answers in the planning of T56, 2026-11-01, all by the recommendation):
  - **`filled` on the sheet.** For an `anywhere` or `sheet` slot, `[[true]]` if the sheet has a stroke that `exportWork()` would take, otherwise `[[false]]`. A sheet with only `choice`, a sheet with no task and an example give `null`. The type is as before, and the moment it changes is as on the workbook: an action changes `filled` before `historychange`, an assignment to `task` and `history` with no event (R37). `app` reads by the binding: for `choice`, its own choice of an option, for the others, `filled`;
  - **the example is the property `workedExample`**, of the type `WorkedExample`. The element shows a task or an example, not both. The example mode:
    - `task` is `undefined`, `history` is empty, there is no `historychange`;
    - `marks` is a `TypeError`, `exportWork()` and `filled` are `null`;
    - `tool` is the pen, with no event, and `undo()` and `clear()` do nothing;
    - the drawing surface takes no input, and the pen mode does not change there. One finger and the pen scroll the sheet, by the browser's scrolling; «Выше» and «Ниже» stayed until T121 (R33, R84);
    - the row of buttons is «Кнопки примера», since T93, and before it «Пример»: «Дальше», «Показ» and, since T94, the zoom's pair — «Выше» and «Ниже» until T121;
  - **the element opens the parts** with its own buttons: «Дальше» and the disclosure button «Показ». For `app`, the property `demoState`, `{ opened, hidden }`, and the event `demochange` only on the learner's press, as with `tool` and `toolchange` (R28). At first no part is open, and the parts do not close one at a time;
  - **the paragraphs and the pictures beside the sheet** stand above the sheet, under the statement: a numbered list of the opened parts in the same column and scrolling. An opened part is scrolled into view at once, with no animation: a picture in an area by its area of the sheet, the other parts by their item of the list;
  - **accessibility:** the reading order is the row of buttons, the statement, the list of parts. A picture in an area is read by its description in the item of the list, and the demo layer stays hidden from the screen reader. A polite `status` region announces an opened part, and the focus stays on «Дальше». «Дальше» refers to the region, and «Показ» to the list, through `aria-controls`. A hidden demo is the attribute `hidden` on the list;
  - **the rows «в реестр spec»** are В52–В54 in table 3 of [RUN.md](../../RUN.md): `filled` on the sheet, the example's property and mode, who opens the parts. `spec` recorded them on 2026-11-01 by `canvas`'s recommendations, as the second amendment to R36 spec, and the types came out in `@altnovatrail/spec/canvas` (the 2026-11-02 amendment to R42; R44).
- **Task:** epic E7, taken by the author's answer to В50 and done on 2026-11-01; its archive is [plan/E7.md](E7.md). T55 took the task's fields, T56 decided the subsection by documents (R42), and T57 built `filled` on the sheet, T58 the example's type and check, T59 the example mode, T60 the demo's parts, T64 the shared loading of pictures, which split off from T60, and T61 the pictures in sheet areas, all done. T62 brought the design together in section 3.
